# Skills · P · part 96

858 entries, play-the-game to players-handbook. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **play-the-game**: Play Final Factory autonomously for the player - read the game state, pick the most useful next goal, carry it out, verify it, report, and repeat. Use when the player says "play the game", "play for me", "keep playing", "just do something useful", "what should we do next", "take over for a while", or gives no specific goal. ([Final-Factory/finalfactory-agent-kit](https://github.com/Final-Factory/finalfactory-agent-kit/tree/HEAD/kit/.claude/skills/play-the-game))

- **play-the-race**: Run the text roguelike the RACE (Run Across a Changing Earth) as its narrator. Do not use for other games, for questions about this repository's code, or for writing detective fiction. ([Klim-101/the-race](https://github.com/Klim-101/the-race/tree/HEAD/plugins/the-race/skills/play-the-race) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **play-through**: Manager loop that hardens the game toward fully-playable end-to-end. It drives the `playtest` primitive in a PLAYTEST to REVIEW to FIX to REPLAY loop: play one session (agent plays a mission via the debug runtime, emits a data.json), adversarially REVIEW that session against a real walkthrough (did it genuinely progress, were the actions sensible, are the findings real - not artifacts of poking the wrong thing), triage the validated blocker/bugs, file + delegate a fix (PR "Fixes #n"), then REPLAY from the advancing frontier - until a mission (then the game) plays through with no new blocker.… ([tommy-xr/shock2quest](https://github.com/tommy-xr/shock2quest/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/play-through) · ★23)

A Playbook is a machine-executable workflow graph: Phases (logical groupings) containing Tasks (atomic executable units with inputs, outputs, dependencies, and success criteria). Trigger this skill when the user wants to design, create, edit, or author a repeatable multi-step agentic workflow. Covers the full interview → validate → save flow, including updating existing playbooks. ([yldgio/vibe-grimoire](https://github.com/yldgio/vibe-grimoire/tree/HEAD/skills/playbook-design) · ★3)

Use before building or redesigning any user interface, when asked how something should behave (confirm vs undo, loading, empty state, animation, focus), when planning a design-system component, or whenever the output must be accessible and non-generic.
